Remove Nth Node From End of List ^tFxinZBV

Q1: In an array, removing the nth from the end is easy — you can just do length - n. Why can't you do the same trick directly with a linked list? ^NLiVaKaf

Q2: What's the minimum information you need to know before you can delete a node from a singly linked list? (Think about what "deleting" actually requires structurally.) ^C6PZncQa

Q3: For the brute force — if you could figure out the total length first, how would that change things? What would your plan be? ^byoDS0ty

/**
 * Definition for singly-linked list.
 * function ListNode(val, next) {
 *     this.val = (val===undefined ? 0 : val)
 *     this.next = (next===undefined ? null : next)
 * }
 */
/**
 * @param {ListNode} head
 * @param {number} n
 * @return {ListNode}
 */
var removeNthFromEnd = function(head, n) {
    // find length
    let current = head
    let lenOfList = 0
    while ( current !== null ) {
        lenOfList +=1
        current = current.next
    }
    
    // delete node by looping over
    if ( lenOfList === n ) // first element to remove 
    {
        head = head.next
        return head
    }

    // otherwise find node to be deleted
    current = head
    let prev = null
    while ( current !== null ) {
        if ( lenOfList === n ) {
            prev.next = current.next
            break    
        }
        lenOfList -= 1
        prev = current
        current = current.next
    }

    return head
}; ^tAVAkYF9

Time Complexity
1. One loop to find length , O(N)
2. Second loop to delte node and break. so at worst if last node to delete then O(N) as well
O(N) + O(N) = O(N)
Space Complexity 
1. Since no array used thus constant O(1) ^udq3bgUx

var removeNthFromEnd = function(head, n) {
    let current = head
    let second = head
    let prev = null
    while( second !== null ) {
        if ( n === 0 ) {
            prev = current
            current = current.next
        }else {
            n -= 1
        }
        second = second.next
    }
    if ( prev === null ) {
        return head.next
    }
    prev.next = current.next
    
    return head
} ^mXxnJ4kN

var removeNthFromEnd = function(head, n) {
    let dummy = new ListNode(0)
    dummy.next = head
    let current = dummy.next
    let second = dummy.next
    let prev = dummy
    while( second !== null ) {
        if ( n === 0 ) {
            prev = current
            current = current.next
        }else {
            n -= 1
        }
        second = second.next
    }
    prev.next = current.next
    return dummy.next
} ^o1179jnn
